We introduce RobustSeiz, an open-source, model-agnostic framework that provides a standardized, reproducible protocol for stress-testing and comparing seizure detectors under controlled, clinically motivated distribution shifts before deployment. We standardize four public scalp-EEG corpora (CHB-MIT, TUSZ, Siena, and SeizeIT1) into BIDS-EEG trees and evaluate subject-independent detectors on held-out splits. Environment, noise, and adversarial transforms are swept over predefined hyperparameter grids. Each run reports sample- and event-level sensitivity, precision, F1, false positives per 24 h, Lead and Lag onset timing, and Monte Carlo dropout predictive agreement. RobustSeiz includes a Dockerized GPU pipeline, experiment registry, and full-evaluation and research-subset modes. We demonstrate the framework with a contemporary seizure detector on TUSZ across the complete implemented shift grid; an AWGN analysis illustrates how perturbation severity changes detection quality, onset timing, and predictive agreement. RobustSeiz provides a shared benchmarking standard for evaluating seizure-detector robustness under realistic clinical stressors, extending pre-deployment assessment beyond clean-data accuracy.

</details>


### [254] [Unlocking Lossless Speedups in LLMs via Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04010)
*Subham Sekhar Sahoo,Lingjie Chen,Khiem Pham,Jonathan Geuter,Chaitanya Dwivedi,Varad Pimpalkhute,Yash Akhauri,Alexander Moreno,Mikhail Yurochkin,Zhenting Wang,Mostafa Elhoushi,Nolan Dey,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness,John Thickstun,Eric Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“扩散增强大语言模型”（Uno），通过解耦自回归权重与轻量级扩散权重，利用扩散蒸馏技术在保持自回归模型质量的同时实现并行多令牌生成，无需独立的草稿模型即可在推理时获得高达3倍的加速，且在多项基准测试中优于现有的扩散LLM和投机解码方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统大语言模型（LLMs）因自回归（AR）结构导致的串行令牌生成速度慢、吞吐量低的瓶颈问题，同时避免现有加速方法（如投机解码需额外草稿模型、扩散LLM牺牲生成质量）的缺陷。

Method: 1. 提出扩散增强LLM架构，将参数解耦为两部分：使用标准下一令牌预测（NTP）目标训练的AR权重，以及通过“扩散蒸馏”阶段训练的轻量级扩散权重，用于并行生成多个令牌。
2. 引入$\Psi$-Spec采样器家族，实现在固定上下文长度下的无损加速和推理时扩展。
3. 支持从头训练或直接增强现有的开源AR LLM。

Result: 1. Uno模型在所有评估的批量大小下，吞吐量均优于领先的投机解码方法。
2. 相比基础AR模型，实现了最高3倍的加速，且在设备支持的最大批量大小下依然有效。
3. 8B参数的Uno模型在智能体工具使用、编码和长上下文推理基准上，全面超越了26B参数的DiffusionGemma及专有模型Mercury 2。

Conclusion: 扩散增强LLM（Uno）成功打破了自回归生成的速度限制，通过引入轻量级扩散机制实现了高效并行推理，且在生成质量上未做妥协，为构建高性能、高质量的生成模型提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) owe much of their success to next-token prediction (NTP), but their autoregressive (AR) structure requires slow, sequential token generation. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce diffusion-augmented LLMs, a new class of models that defines an AR model distribution while using diffusion to draw multiple tokens in parallel from that distribution. We decouple the parameters of these models into two sets: AR weights, trained using the standard NTP objective, and lightweight diffusion weights, trained to generate multiple tokens simultaneously. The diffusion weights are learned through a simple Diffusion Distillation phase that adds negligible overhead to existing LLM training pipelines. We also introduce $Ψ$-Spec, a family of samplers that enables lossless acceleration and inference-time scaling at a fixed context length. Unlike speculative decoding, our method requires no separate draft model. Unlike diffusion LLMs (d-LLMs), it accelerates generation without sacrificing the quality of the underlying AR model. The resulting models, called Uno, can be trained from scratch or built by augmenting existing open-weight AR LLMs. Uno achieves higher throughput than leading speculative-decoding methods at every evaluated batch size and delivers up to $3\times$ speedups over the base AR model, including at the largest batch size supported by the device. Notably, our 8B Uno model outperforms the leading open d-LLM, the 26B DiffusionGemma, and the proprietary Mercury 2 across all evaluated benchmarks in agentic tool use, coding, and long-context reasoning. We release code and checkpoints at: https://s-sahoo.github.io/uno/

</details>


### [255] [A location-invariant estimator of extremal quantile treatment effects for heavy-tailed distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04018)
*Xin Yu,Shuwei Huang,Jicheng Liu,Jielin Tang,Bolin Wang,Yunxiao Zhang,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的极值分位数处理效应（QTE）估计量，通过结合逆倾向得分加权的位置不变EVI估计量和基于差分的插值方案，解决了现有估计量在位置平移下不不变的问题，并证明了其一致性和渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重尾潜在结果的应用中，现有极值QTE估计量依赖外推和因果极值指数（EVI）估计，但即使总体QTE具有位置不变性，这些估计量在潜在结果分布发生共同位置平移时并不保持不变，导致估计偏差。

Method: 分两步改进：1）利用逆倾向得分加权将Fraga位置不变EVI估计量适配到因果推断场景；2）用基于差分的插值方案替代原有外推公式，使位置参数在计算分位数差值时自动抵消，从而构建位置不变的QTE估计量。

Result: 建立了所提极值QTE估计量的一致性和渐近正态性，提供了有效的方差估计量以实现渐近有效推断；模拟研究验证了该方法的位置不变性、阈值稳定性及置信区间覆盖率。

Conclusion: 提出的新估计量成功解决了现有方法在位置平移下的不变性问题，为重尾分布下极端分位数处理效应的可靠推断提供了理论保障和实践工具。

Abstract: Quantile treatment effects (QTEs) measure the effect of a treatment on the distribution of an outcome, and their estimation at extreme quantile levels is of central interest in applications where the target quantiles lie far beyond the range of the data. For heavy-tailed potential outcomes, existing extremal QTE estimators rely on extrapolation combined with a causal extreme value index (EVI) estimator, but the resulting estimator is not invariant under a common location shift of the potential outcome distributions, even though the population QTE is. We address this issue in two steps. First, we adapt the location-invariant Fraga estimator of the EVI to the causal setting using inverse propensity score weighting. Second, we replace the original extrapolation formula with a difference-based scheme, under which the location parameter cancels when quantile differences are taken. The resulting QTE estimator is therefore location invariant. We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed extremal QTE estimators, and provide a consistent variance estimator, leading to asymptotically valid inference. A simulation study confirms the location invariance, the stability with respect to the threshold, and the coverage of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [256] [Subspace Inference Enables Efficient Active Reward Learning from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04066)
*Yutai Zhou,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PreferenceEKF方法，通过将主动偏好学习构建为序列贝叶斯滤波问题，并在低维参数子空间内利用扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）进行序列推断，从而高效追踪奖励模型的不确定性，显著提升了RLHF的样本效率、运行速度和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）中奖励模型样本效率低下的问题，特别是克服大型神经网络奖励模型在主动学习中难以进行有效不确定性量化（Uncertainty Quantification）的关键挑战，避免全参数空间后验推断带来的巨大计算开销。

Method: 提出PreferenceEKF框架，将主动偏好学习视为序列贝叶斯滤波问题。该方法不直接在全神经网络参数空间进行后验推断，而是在低维参数子空间内利用扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）执行序列推断，随着新偏好查询的到来持续更新奖励模型后验分布，并据此 scalable 地采样神经网络参数以计算主动学习的采集函数。

Result: 在D4RL和V-D4RL基准测试上的实验表明，相较于其他贝叶斯深度学习方案，该方法在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性及校准度（calibration）方面均表现更优，且基于此学到的奖励模型能引导出具有竞争力的离线强化学习策略性能。

Conclusion: 可扩展的贝叶斯方法在基于偏好的奖励建模及RLHF应用中具有巨大潜力，PreferenceEKF为高效的不确定性量化和主动学习提供了一条可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/yutaizhou/bnn_pref.

</details>


### [257] [Conditioning Degenerate Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04090)
*Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对条件生成模型在扩散系数奇异或密度不光滑时的问题，提出基于因果最优传输的近似损失函数方法，以最小熵控制进行引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前条件生成模型严重依赖分数函数进行训练引导，但在扩散系数奇异或底层条件密度不存在/不光滑的情况下，传统方法失效。

Method: 利用因果最优传输理论，通过条件扩散过程的可料表示性质（martingale问题适定性）来定义近似损失函数，识别最小熵控制进行引导。

Result: 在最小假设条件下，成功定义了适用于奇异扩散系数或非光滑密度场景的近似损失函数，实现了有效的生成引导。

Conclusion: 基于因果最优传输的方法为条件生成模型在复杂数学条件下的训练引导提供了新的理论框架和解决方案。

Abstract: Current conditioned generative models heavily rely on score functions for guidance during training. When the generative model is a diffusion process with a singular diffusion coefficient and the underlying (conditional) densities either do not exist or are not smooth, we use causal optimal transport to define \emph{approximate} loss functions that identify a minimum-entropy control for guidance under minimal assumptions. Our approach relies on causal optimal transport and its characterization through the predictable representation property of (conditioned) diffusion processes whose associated martingale problem is well posed, à la Üstünel.

</details>


### [258] [Hardware-Aware FP4 FlashAttention-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04105)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对 Blackwell 4 位浮点（FP4）张量核心在注意力机制中因 softmax 转换和芯片内依赖导致性能瓶颈的问题，提出了 Direct-P 非因果推理方法和一种因果路径，通过直接量化前向传播并重构概率，显著提升了吞吐量和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blackwell 的 FP4 张量核心在处理注意力机制时，由于矩阵乘积缩小，softmax 转换和芯片内依赖成为性能瓶颈，无法自动加速注意力计算。

Method: 提出 Direct-P 方法用于非因果推理，将分数直接映射为 FP4 概率；设计因果路径，将前向量化直接传递到反向传播，通过保存的量化查询和键重构概率，并使用 FP8 梯度操作数。

Result: 在 NVIDIA GB200 上，Direct-P 实现高达 2.13 倍于 BF16 的前向吞吐量；因果路径加速完整单 GPU 80 亿参数更新达 1.14 倍；分布式训练保留 FP8 概率和值，而 MXFP4 概率/值训练轨迹均发散。

Conclusion: 通过 Direct-P 和因果路径设计，有效解决了 FP4 在注意力机制中的瓶颈问题，显著提升了推理和训练效率，但 MXFP4 在概率/值训练中仍存在稳定性问题。

Abstract: Blackwell's 4-bit floating-point (FP4) tensor cores do not automatically make attention faster because softmax conversion and on-chip dependencies dominate once its matrix products shrink. We address this with \emph{Direct-P} for noncausal inference and a causal path that passes the forward quantization directly into backward. Direct-P maps scores directly to FP4 probabilities and reaches up to 2.13$\times$ the bfloat16 (BF16) forward throughput on an NVIDIA GB200. The causal path reconstructs probabilities from saved quantized queries and keys and uses 8-bit floating-point (FP8) gradient operands, accelerating a complete single-GPU 8-billion-parameter update by up to 1.14$\times$. Matched distributed training retains FP8 probabilities and values; every tested MXFP4 probability/value training trajectory diverges.

</details>


### [259] [Constant regret in general games via higher-order optimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04113)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HOOD的解耦学习算法，通过在提升的策略空间上结合折扣高阶预测器与熵正则化，有效抑制了博弈过程中的剧烈振荡，从而在任意N人正则形式博弈中实现了$O(N^3\log^2 K)$的个体遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决以往在一般博弈中尝试实现常数遗憾（constant regret）时遇到的关键障碍，即由诱导博弈序列产生的大幅振荡问题。

Method: 提出高阶乐观折扣算法（HOOD），作为乐观跟随正则化领导者（OptFTRL）的变体。该方法结合了两个核心要素：一是针对博弈策略空间适当“提升”后的折扣$(N+1)$阶预测器，二是熵正则化。这种组合旨在以受控方式抑制序列振荡。

Result: 在任意具有$N$个玩家且每个玩家最多$K$个动作的正则形式博弈中，该算法保证了$O(N^3\log^2 K)$的个体遗憾，且该界限在整个博弈时间范围内一致成立。

Conclusion: HOOD算法成功克服了先前方法的局限性，显著降低了遗憾界限（相较于同期Liu等人工作的$O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$），证明了高阶乐观与特定正则化结合在抑制振荡和降低遗憾方面的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce an uncoupled learning algorithm which, when employed by all players of an arbitrary $N$-player normal form game with up to $K$ actions per player, guarantees $O(N^3\log^2 K)$ individual regret, uniformly over the horizon of play. The proposed algorithm - which we call higher-order optimism with discounting (HOOD) is a variant of optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OptFTRL) that combines a discounted $(N+1)$-th order predictor with entropic regularization over a suitable "lifting" of the game's strategy space. This combination of ingredients is purposefully designed to dampen large oscillations of the induced sequence of play in a controlled manner, removing in this way a key stumbling block of previous attempts to achieve constant regret in general games. Our approach bears several striking similarities to the concurrent - and completely independent - work of Liu, Farina, and Ozdaglar (arXiv:2608.31166), who very recently derived an $O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$ regret bound through the use of higher-order optimism and an exponential moving average estimator.

</details>


### [260] [Prospective Coding Improves Learning in Deep Continuous-Time Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04134)
*Shivang Rawat,Mirko Morello,Flaviano Morone,David J. Heeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归正交滤波器（RQFs）及“前瞻性输入”机制，解决了深层连续时间循环网络中信号延迟和梯度衰减问题，在语音和长序列任务上实现了参数高效的高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深层连续时间循环网络中，时间积分机制导致的自下而上信号延迟和自上而下误差衰减的失效模式，特别是在深层堆叠结构中。

Method: 1. 从能量模型推导RQF动力学，将其设计为可控频率和带宽的带通滤波器（对角状态空间模型的特例）；2. 引入无参数的双抽头更新机制，使每一层的自下而上输入具有“前瞻性”；3. 将该修正推广至通用对角SSMs，并在完整BPTT和仅空间反向传播下进行评估。

Result: 在RQFs、S5和ORGaNICs模型中，前瞻性变体在完整BPTT下均匹配或优于非前瞻性对照；仅空间反向传播下缓解了深度依赖的梯度衰减。具体性能：31.9k参数的6层RQF在Speech Commands上达96.09%准确率；64宽度的6层RQF在Path-X任务上达83.56%。

Conclusion: RQFs是一种参数高效的循环基底，而前瞻性输入编码是解决深层连续时间循环网络输入侧缺陷的有效修正方案。

Abstract: Temporal integration gives continuous-time recurrent networks memory, but in deep stacks it also delays bottom-up signals and attenuates top-down errors. We develop Recursive Quadrature Filters (RQFs), biologically motivated complex-valued temporal filters that are a special case of diagonal state-space models (SSMs), and ask whether this failure mode can be addressed by making each layer's bottom-up input prospective. Starting from an energy model, we derive the RQF dynamics and show that each RQF is a band-pass filter whose learnable parameters control its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We then make each layer's bottom-up input prospective using a parameter-free two-tap update that leaves the recurrent transition and parallel scan unchanged. We extend this correction to general diagonal SSMs and show that it mitigates depth-dependent gradient attenuation when temporal gradients are truncated, i.e., spatial-only backpropagation. We evaluate the intervention in RQFs, S5, and ORGaNICs (a nonlinear gated RNN) trained using full backpropagation through time (BPTT) and spatial-only backpropagation. Under full BPTT, prospective variants match or outperform their non-prospective controls in every model and configuration. A non-residual width-32 six-layer RQF reaches 96.09% accuracy on raw-audio Speech Commands with 31.9k parameters; a width-64 six-layer RQF reaches 83.56% on the 16,384-step Path-X task. These results identify RQFs as a parameter-efficient recurrent substrate and prospective-input coding as an input-side correction for deep continuous-time recurrent networks.

</details>


### [261] [A Low-Cost, Open Platform for End-to-End Autonomous Driving on a Miniature Ackermann Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04147)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本的开源端到端自动驾驶实验平台，结合物理车辆、打印赛道、数据采集工具、轨迹配准和Webots数字孪生，实现仿真与实车实验的闭环验证。通过命令条件的行为克隆方法，在实车和仿真中均取得良好性能，并利用数字孪生生成的合成数据结合sim-to-real图像翻译器进一步提升策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶研究缺乏低成本、可复现的端到端实验平台，难以连接仿真方法与实际执行。因此，本文旨在构建一个开源平台，支持可控的仿真实验与实车验证，促进sim-to-real研究的可重复性。

Method: 1. 构建包含物理Ackermann车辆、打印城市赛道、数据采集工具、轨迹配准和Webots数字孪生的实验平台。2. 实现命令条件的行为克隆方法，神经策略接收车载摄像头图像和高级导航命令，输出转向和速度。3. 在实车和仿真中进行闭环实验评估。4. 利用数字孪生生成的合成数据，结合学习的sim-to-real图像翻译器，训练更高容量的策略。

Result: 1. 实车闭环实验中，学习策略的平均横向误差为6.1 cm，接近人类演示的4.7 cm。2. 在数字孪生中，摄像头视野从58度扩大到120度，平均横向误差从35.6 cm降至3.3 cm。3. 使用合成数据结合sim-to-real图像翻译器训练的高容量策略，是唯一能完成所有四条赛道路线的配置，而紧凑基线和仅用真实数据训练的网络完成较少路线。

Conclusion: 该开源平台为sim-to-real研究提供了实用的测试平台，并建立了命令条件模仿学习的初始基线。平台已公开，以支持可重复的自动驾驶研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-cost, open experimental platform for research in end-to-end autonomous driving with miniature Ackermann vehicles. The platform combines a physical vehicle, a printed urban track, data collection tools, trajectory registration, and a Webots digital twin, enabling controlled experiments that connect simulation-based autonomous-driving methods to real-world execution. As a first baseline, we implement command-conditioned behavior cloning, in which a neural policy receives an on-board camera image and a high-level navigation command and outputs steering and speed. The system is evaluated both on the physical vehicle and in simulation. In real closed-loop experiments, the learned policy follows lanes and executes commanded turns, reaching a mean cross-track error of 6.1 cm with respect to the reference route, close to the 4.7 cm observed in human demonstrations. In the digital twin, camera field of view has a strong effect on performance, reducing the mean cross-track error from 35.6 to 3.3 cm when widened from 58 to 120 degrees. Using the digital twin to generate synthetic driving data and a learned sim-to-real image translator to reduce the appearance gap, we further show that a higher-capacity policy trained on this synthetic data combined with real demonstrations is the only configuration that completes all four track routes in closed loop, whereas the compact baseline and the same network trained on real data alone complete fewer. These results establish the open platform as a practical testbed for sim-to-real studies and provide an initial command-conditioned imitation-learning baseline; we release it to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [262] [Robust PAC Learning of Concurrent Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04189)
*Angel Y. He,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对具有转移不确定性的广义和并发随机博弈（CSG）的PAC学习框架，该框架能计算社会福利最优的$\varepsilon$-纳什均衡（NE），或在精确NE不存在时提供证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决广义和并发随机博弈中因转移不确定性导致的纳什均衡存在性挑战，并填补该领域缺乏PAC学习框架的空白。

Method: 算法维护基于数据的$L^1$转移核置信集，求解鲁棒CSG以计算社会福利最优的$\varepsilon$-NE；引入基于鲁棒MDP的探索机制驱动联合状态 - 动作覆盖，并提出“纳什边际”（Nash margin）表征以原理性地推理均衡存在性。

Result: 在最小可达性条件下，算法以多项式数量的轨迹样本终止，样本复杂度为$\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$；基准测试显示其性能接近最优，能正确处理均衡的（非）存在性，且实证样本复杂度与理论一致。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅能在不确定环境下高效学习近似最优均衡，还能通过严格的数学证明确认精确均衡的不存在性，为随机博弈的鲁棒学习提供了理论保证和实践有效性。

Abstract: We introduce the first Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning framework for general-sum concurrent stochastic games (CSGs) with transition uncertainty, while addressing the challenge of Nash equilibrium (NE) existence. Our algorithm maintains data-driven $L^1$ confidence sets over transition kernels and solves a robust CSG to compute a social-welfare optimal $\varepsilon$-NE, using a robust MDP-based exploration mechanism to drive joint state-action coverage. Crucially, we introduce a Nash margin characterisation that enables principled reasoning about equilibrium existence: the framework either returns an $\varepsilon$-approximate NE whose social-welfare value is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal, or provides a sound certificate that no exact NE exists. Under a minimum reachability condition $p_{\mathrm{reach}}>0$ over relevant state-action pairs, the algorithm terminates after a polynomial number of trajectory samples, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$. Empirical results on benchmark CSGs demonstrate near-optimal performance, correct handling of equilibrium (non-)existence, and sample complexity consistent with theory.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Occupancy-based Quantile Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03104)
*Zihao Shi,Huajun Xi,Bingyi Jing,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于占用率的量化风险控制（OQRC）方法，通过将损失空间划分为有序校准损失的区间并估计测试损失分布，解决了现有量化风险控制方法过于保守或缺乏有限样本保证的问题，实现了紧致且收敛速度为 $\mathcal{O}_p(n^{-1/2})$ 的风险控制边界，并在实验中显著降低了风险差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量化风险控制扩展虽然适应了更广泛的风险概念，但存在两个主要局限：一是方法过于保守，二是缺乏严格的有限样本保证。为了克服这些缺陷，需要一种既能提供紧致风险控制边界又能保证有限样本有效性的新方法。

Method: 提出基于占用率的量化风险控制（OQRC）框架。核心思想是将风险控制建模为有限占用问题：利用有序校准损失对损失空间进行划分，估计测试损失在各区间（bins）内的分布，并通过每个区间内达到的最大损失来界定风险上界。随后，选择参数 $\lambda$ 使得该上界以高概率 $1-\delta$ 不超过预设阈值 $\alpha$。

Result: 理论上证明了OQRC具有有限样本保证，其风险控制边界是紧致的，并以 $\mathcal{O}_p(n^{-1/2})$ 的速率收敛至最优边界。实验结果表明，该方法在常见基准测试上将风险差距（risk gap）降低了高达 78.64%。

Conclusion: OQRC 成功解决了现有方法在保守性和理论保证之间的权衡问题，提供了一种具有严格有限样本有效性且边界紧致的量化风险控制方案，显著提升了机器学习模型部署的安全性。

Abstract: Conformal risk control is an emerging framework for the safe deployment of machine learning models with finite-sample guarantees. To accommodate a broader class of risk notions, quantile risk control extends this framework to quantile-based risk measures. However, existing methods either suffer from excessive conservatism or lack rigorous finite-sample guarantees. To address these limitations, we introduce Occupancy-based Quantile Risk Control (OQRC), a novel method that provides tight risk control bounds with finite-sample validity. Our key idea is to formulate risk control as a finite-occupancy problem by partitioning the loss space with the ordered calibration losses. Specifically, we estimate the distribution of test losses across the resulting bins and upper-bound the risk by the maximum loss attained within each bin. We then select the parameter $λ$ such that this upper bound does not exceed a predefined threshold $α$ with high probability $1-δ$. Theoretically, we establish a finite-sample guarantee showing that OQRC yields tight risk control bounds that converge to the optimal bounds at a provable rate of $\mathcal{O}_ p(n^{-1/2})$. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, reducing the risk gap by up to 78.64\% on common benchmarks.

</details>


### [264] [A Closed-Form Formula for Consistent Lipschitz Regression on Metric Spaces with Sparse Neural Network Realizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03129)
*Ruiyang Hong,Hrad Ghoukasian,Anastasis Kratsios*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单的闭式“两阶段”组合公式，用于从噪声观测中重构未知的Lipschitz函数，实现了零优化误差的高概率一致恢复保证，并在函数空间、参数空间和前向传播三个方面证明了其最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典机器学习方法（如KRRs和SVRs）具有计算和分析上的可处理性，因为它们具有闭式表达式或通过最小化凸训练目标获得估计器，而深度神经网络通常不具备这些特性。本文旨在解决这一问题，为深度神经网络提供类似的闭式解和理论保证。

Method: 引入一个简单的闭式“两阶段”组合公式$\hat{f}$，用于在度量空间$(\mathcal X,ρ)$上从$N$个i.i.d.噪声观测中重构未知的Lipschitz函数$f:\mathcal{X}\to \mathbb{R}$。该方法不依赖于近似ERM的oracle访问，而是通过闭式公式直接实现函数重构。

Result: 1) 主要结果：提供了高概率的一致（$L^{\infty}$）恢复保证，同时控制近似误差和统计误差，且优化误差为零。2) 次要结果：在三个方面证明了公式的最优性：函数空间（在Ahlfors正则度量空间上达到最优fat-shattering维度）、参数空间（参数依赖具有最大数值稳定性）、前向传播（输入依赖的正则性与目标函数$f$的Lipschitz常数匹配）。当$\mathcal X=[0,1]^d$配备$\ell^\infty$范数时，$\hat{f}$可实现深度为$\mathcal{O}(\log(N))$、非零参数为$\mathcal{O}(N)$的算法ReLU-MLP和精确ReLU多头Transformer实现。

Conclusion: 本文提出的闭式公式为深度神经网络提供了类似经典机器学习方法的理论保证和计算可处理性，同时在函数逼近、参数稳定性和计算效率方面达到了最优，为深度学习的理论分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Several classical machine-learning methods, such as KRRs and SVRs, are both computationally and analytically tractable since their estimators either admit closed-form expressions or are obtained by minimizing convex training objectives; neither feature is generally available for deep neural networks. We address this by introducing a simple closed-form ``two-stage'' compositional formula $\hat{f}$ for reconstructing an unknown Lipschitz function $f:\mathcal{X}\to \mathbb{R}$ on a metric space $(\mathcal X,ρ)$ from $N$ i.i.d. noisy observations.
  Our main result is a high-probability uniform ($L^{\infty}$) recovery guarantee that jointly controls approximation and statistical errors while enjoying an optimization error of zero; in particular, we do not assume oracle access to an approximate ERM. Our secondary main results establish the optimality of our formula in three complementary senses. 1) Function space: On Ahlfors-regular metric spaces, the hypothesis class parameterized by our formula attains the optimal fat-shattering dimension. 2) Parameter space: Its dependence on the parameters is maximally numerically stable, in the sense that a smaller approximation error cannot be achieved with a smaller Lipschitz dependence on the model parameters. 3) Forward pass: Its dependence on the input is maximally regular, matching the Lipschitz constant of the target function $f$. When $\mathcal X=[0,1]^d$ is equipped with the $\ell^\infty$ norm, $\hat{f}$ admits algorithmic ReLU-MLP and exact ReLU-multi-head transformer realizations of depth $\mathcal{O}(\log(N))$ with $\mathcal{O}(N)$ nonzero parameters.

</details>


### [265] [ALRA: Adaptive Local Relational Alignment for Logit-Based Pre-training Distillation of Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03355)
*Quang Hoang Trung,Quang Huu Hieu,Nguyen Van Hoang Phuc,Vo Nguyen Le Duy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为自适应局部关系对齐（ALRA）的知识蒸馏方法，用于自回归语言模型。该方法通过结合学生模型提出的候选词和教师模型的锚点词，动态调整候选词数量，并在选定的词汇区域和剩余词汇区域内分别匹配相对分布，从而改进了传统的基于logit的全局对齐方法。实验表明，ALRA在多个零样本基准测试中优于现有蒸馏基线和无蒸馏预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于logit的知识蒸馏方法通常在整个词汇表上对齐教师和学生模型的下一个词分布，但这种全局目标忽略了可能词选项之间的相对偏好。现有的局部方法往往仅从教师或学生模型中选择候选词，教师单独选择可能会遗漏学生认为可能的词，而学生单独选择在训练早期可能依赖不准确的排名。

Method: 提出自适应局部关系对齐（ALRA）框架，在每个有效预测位置，学生提出可能的词，同时包含教师最可能的词作为锚点。ALRA根据教师在该候选集内相对于当前批次分布概率的广度调整选择词的数量。自适应局部分散保留质量匹配项，并分别在选定和剩余词汇区域内匹配相对词分布。学生加权成对关系对齐强调高概率词对，且学生概率差距较小，给予低概率或明显分离的词对较少权重。

Result: 在The Pile数据集上，使用随机初始化的200M和500M参数学生模型进行实验，在九个零样本基准测试中，平均准确率分别为36.62%和37.40%。ALRA比最强的竞争蒸馏基线分别高出0.94和0.83个百分点，比无蒸馏预训练分别提高2.31和2.91个百分点。

Conclusion: ALRA通过结合学生和教师模型的候选词选择，并动态调整候选词数量，有效改进了知识蒸馏中的局部关系对齐，显著提升了学生模型在多个基准测试中的性能。

Abstract: Logit-based knowledge distillation for autoregressive language models usually aligns teacher and student next-token distributions over the entire vocabulary. However, this global objective overlooks relative preferences among likely token alternatives. Existing local approaches often select candidate tokens from either the teacher or the student alone. Teacher-only selection can miss tokens that the student considers likely, while student-only selection can rely on an inaccurate ranking early in training. We propose Adaptive Local Relational Alignment (ALRA), a position-specific framework combining student proposals with teacher guidance. At each valid prediction position, the student proposes likely tokens, while the teacher's most probable token is included as an anchor. ALRA adjusts the number of selected tokens according to how broadly the teacher distributes probability within this candidate set relative to the current batch. Adaptive Local Divergence retains the mass-matching term and separately matches the relative token distributions within the selected and remaining vocabulary regions. Unlike the exact full-vocabulary decomposition, it replaces the teacher-mass coefficients of the two conditional terms with unit coefficients, preventing either term from being downweighted solely because its region has low teacher probability. Student-Weighted Pairwise Relational Alignment emphasizes high-probability token pairs with small student probability gaps and gives less weight to unlikely or clearly separated pairs. Experiments on The Pile with randomly initialized 200M- and 500M-parameter students across nine zero-shot benchmarks yield average accuracies of 36.62% and 37.40%. ALRA exceeds the strongest competing distillation baseline by 0.94 and 0.83 percentage points and improves over pre-training without distillation by 2.31 and 2.91 points, respectively.

</details>


### [266] [Towards a Statistical Understanding of Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03501)
*Siyuan He,Bokai Yang,Jie Hu,Ziwen Gao,Yuhong Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从统计学习理论角度分析混合专家模型（MoE），提出将 MoE 视为局部聚合形式，推导了密集和稀疏路由的 Oracle 风险界，并解释了共享专家如何提取公共预测结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 MoE 架构在现代大规模模型中日益重要，但其设计选择（特别是路由、稀疏激活和共享专家）的统计作用尚未被完全理解，现有理论主要集中在参数化或正确指定的 MoE 模型上。

Method: 将 MoE 视为局部聚合形式，推导密集和稀疏路由的 Oracle 风险界，分离近似误差、专家学习误差和路由估计误差，并通过输入空间几何解释门控机制，分析共享专家的作用。

Result: 推导了学习密集和稀疏路由的 Oracle 风险界，证明稀疏 Top-K 路由可在控制每输入计算量的同时保留局部聚合优势，共享专家可提取公共预测结构使路由专家专注于残差局部变化。

Conclusion: 提供了一个统一的统计框架，通过输入依赖的专家聚合理解 MoE，其中专家专业化和计算权衡由局部预测结构决定。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures increase model capacity by combining a collection of expert predictors through input-dependent routing, while often activating only a small subset of experts for each input. Despite their growing importance in modern large-scale models, the statistical roles of their design choices, especially routing, sparse activation, and shared experts, remain only partially understood, as existing theory has largely focused on parametric or correctly specified MoE models. In this paper, we view MoE as a form of localized aggregation and show how this localization reshapes the approximation-estimation-computation tradeoff. We derive oracle risk bounds for learning dense and sparse routing with evolving experts, separating approximation, expert-learning, and router-estimation errors, and characterize how sparse Top-K routing can retain the benefits of localized aggregation while controlling per-input computation. We also interpret gating through the geometry of input space, relating routing performance to regions of local expert advantage, and show how shared experts, as adopted in architectures such as DeepSeekMoE, can extract common predictive structure so that routed experts focus on residual local variation. Together, these results provide a unified statistical framework for understanding MoE through input-dependent expert aggregation, in which expert specialization and computational tradeoffs are governed by local predictive structure.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [Structure and Implementation of New Practical English Textbooks Driven by Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02981)
*Ya Wang,Lei Zhang,Xueguang Yang,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于人工智能的五层架构实用英语教材，通过个性化学习路径和实时反馈，显著提升了非英语专业大学生的单元完成率、口语成绩，并减少了教师批改时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正在将应用英语材料从固定的纸质序列转变为能够诊断学习者、推荐任务并提供形成性反馈的自适应学习系统，旨在解决传统静态教材缺乏个性化和实时反馈的问题。

Method: 提出包含知识映射、学习者画像、任务生成、反馈编排和教师端治理的五层架构，并在186名非英语专业本科生中进行了为期八周的原型系统教学实验，与静态数字教材进行对比分析。

Result: 相比静态数字教材，该AI驱动系统使单元完成准确率从72.4%提升至84.9%，口语任务平均分提高10.8分，教师批改时间减少31.6%。

Conclusion: AI驱动的教材能够在保持课程稳定性的同时，提供个性化学习路径、丰富的练习材料和可追溯的课堂数据，有效优化英语教学效果。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is changing the form of applied English materials from fixed paper sequences to adaptive learning systems that can diagnose learners, recommend tasks, and provide formative feedback. This paper studies the structure and application of a new practical English textbook driven by artificial intelligence. A five-layer architecture is proposed: knowledge mapping, learner profiling, task generation, feedback orchestration, and teacher-side governance. A prototype was tested on 186 non-English-major undergraduates for eight weeks of teaching. Compared with a static digital textbook, the proposed system increased the unit completion accuracy from 72.4% to 84.9%, raised the average score for speaking tasks by 10.8 points, and reduced the teacher's correction time by 31.6%. Therefore, an AI-driven textbook can maintain the stability of the curriculum while providing personalised learning paths, rich practice materials and traceable classroom data.

</details>


### [268] [MasterControl Seventeen Every Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03209)
*MasterControl AI Lab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受管控的企业分析方法：由语言模型理解意图，确定性策略执行预批准的分析程序。实验显示，相比让模型在运行时规划（330次全失败），该策略执行方法在110次测试中实现了100%的准确率和证据完整性，证明了在限定分析类内受控方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决企业分析中语言模型直接生成代码或规划任务时存在的不确定性、不可复现性及潜在的安全风险，确保分析结果的准确性、可解释性和合规性。

Method: 采用“语言模型解释意图 + 确定性策略执行预批准程序”的架构。语言模型仅负责语义理解，不涉及具体执行逻辑；确定性策略基于固定规则选择并运行包含关系运算、聚合、比较、窗口函数、排序及相似度计算的分析程序，确保结果可复现且附带证据。

Result: 在440次运行中，三个8B模型尝试运行时规划（生成SQL和选择工具）在330次测试中无一完全符合“答案加证据”的契约；而由策略执行的分析器在110次测试中全部成功匹配（110/110）。

Conclusion: 在特定的配置和分析类别下，受管控的确定性执行方法显著优于运行时自主规划方法。这并非否定智能体在其他设计下的潜力，但强调了在企业级应用中，通过限制表达空间换取确定性和可靠性的重要性。

Abstract: We study a governed approach to enterprise analytics: a language model interprets the question, while deterministic policy selects and runs a pre-approved analytical program that returns both results and evidence. We show that this restriction can remain expressive within a defined analytical class, using relational operations plus aggregation, comparison, windows, ranking, and similarity. Fixed meaning, policy, data, and execution rules also make results replayable. Across 440 runs, three 8B models generated SQL and selected tools at runtime, while Qwen3-8B interpreted intent only and policy executed the approved program. None of 330 runtime-planning episodes matched the full answer-and-evidence contract across all test datasets; the policy-executed analyzer matched 110 of 110. This is a configuration-specific result, not evidence that runtime agents cannot succeed under other designs.

</details>


### [269] [Speculative Macro Commit for Faster Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03236)
*Zeyu Liu,Souvik Kundu,Peter A. Beerel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Speculative Macro Commit(SMC)机制，通过两层级代理系统（权威模型+推测模型）预执行多步动作链，在保持准确率的同时显著降低工具使用LLM代理的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用LLM代理在序列动作-观察回合中消耗大量墙钟时间，每个工具调用、环境转换和观察都会延迟后续决策，需要减少推理和执行延迟。

Method: 设计两层级系统：大型权威模型生成官方轨迹，快速推测模型在隔离环境快照上持续预测并执行未来动作链；从训练轨迹中挖掘重复多动作骨架存入宏库，运行时匹配推测动作链；当权威模型的下一步工具调用与推测动作匹配时，提交预执行的剩余步骤及观察结果到官方轨迹。

Result: 在τ²-Bench Telecom子集上，相比SA基线延迟降低10.23%，相比顺序执行降低18.59%；在AppWorld上，相比SA基线墙钟时间降低7.7%，相比顺序执行降低44.9%，任务完成率略有下降。

Conclusion: SMC提供了一种实用的多步推测执行复用方法，能够有效降低代理延迟，超越单步推测动作的限制。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents spend wall-clock time not only on model inference but also in serial action--observation turns, where each tool call, environment transition, and observation can delay subsequent decisions. We introduce \textbf{Speculative Macro Commit} (SMC), a runtime mechanism for a two-tier agent system: a large authoritative actor model produces the official trajectory, while a faster speculative drafter model continuously predicts and executes future action chains on an isolated environment snapshot. SMC mines recurring multi-action skeletons from training traces and stores them in a macro library used to match against action chains predicted by the drafter at runtime. When the actor's next tool call matches the first drafted action, SMC commits the remaining pre-executed draft steps, together with their observations, to the official trajectory. Using Qwen3.5-27B INT4 as the authoritative actor model and Qwen3.5-4B as the speculative drafter model, SMC matches the sequential agent's overall accuracy while reducing latency by 10.23\% over the Speculative Actions (SA) baseline and 18.59\% over sequential execution on the $τ^2$-Bench Telecom subset. On AppWorld, SMC reduces wall time by 7.7\% over SA baseline and 44.9\% over sequential execution, with a small reduction in task completion. Overall, SMC provides a practical way to reuse multi-step speculative execution and reduce agent latency beyond single-step speculative actions. Our code is publicly available \href{https://github.com/zeyuliu1037/speculative-macro-commit}{\textcolor{magenta}{here}}.

</details>


### [270] [Fresh Memory, Stale Plans: Dependency-Scoped Validation for Distributed LLM-Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03340)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对分布式LLM代理团队中存在的“陈旧计划执行”问题（即状态虽新但计划依据已失效），本文提出了PlanFence协议。该协议通过依赖范围的动作验证机制，确保执行器仅验证影响当前动作的关键记录，从而在动态变化环境中避免无效操作。实验表明，PlanFence在高频率变更场景下能完全避免无效动作，且在协调停滞和验证开销上优于传统仅依赖状态新鲜度的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决分布式LLM代理协作中的“陈旧计划执行”难题：即使共享事实是最新的，代理仍可能基于过时的需求推导出的计划执行动作，导致系统行为无效或不一致。现有的仅依赖状态新鲜度（freshness-only）的机制无法保证计划授权的有效性。

Method: 提出PlanFence，一种依赖范围的动作验证协议。其核心机制包括：1）计划必须明确引用其所依据的具体公共记录；2）执行器仅验证那些能影响待定外部动作的记录；3）当验证不完整时，触发重新规划或阻塞动作，而非盲目执行。

Result: 在30个受控实时工作流测试中，仅依赖新鲜度的执行器在所有任务中均执行了过时计划，而PlanFence在所有任务中均成功完成且无无效动作。回放实验显示：在低变更率下，主动同步协调停滞更低；而在高变更率下，PlanFence避免了重复的路径协调开销，并随着共享键空间增大，避免了对无关状态的验证。

Conclusion: PlanFence在受控环境下证明了其在系统安全性和成本效率上的优势，能有效防止陈旧计划执行。研究强调这是关于安全性和系统开销的成果，而非通用任务准确性的提升，为构建高可靠分布式LLM代理系统提供了新的验证范式。

Abstract: Distributed LLM-agent teams can read the latest shared facts and still act on an obsolete plan. A planner may derive an action from requirement $r_3$, another agent may commit $r_4$, and an executor may receive $r_4$ without replacing the plan derived from $r_3$. We call this \emph{stale-plan execution}: state freshness does not establish that the plan authorizing an action remains valid. We introduce PlanFence, a dependency-scoped action-validation protocol. Plans cite the exact public records they used, and an executor validates only the records that can affect the pending external action, replanning once or blocking when validation is incomplete. In 30 controlled live workflows with a post-plan revision, a freshness-only executor acts on the obsolete plan in every task, whereas PlanFence completes all tasks without an invalid action. Controlled replay reveals two conditional boundaries: proactive synchronization yields lower coordination stall at low churn, while PlanFence avoids repeated update-path coordination as churn grows and avoids validating unrelated state as the shared keyspace grows. These are controlled safety and systems-cost results, not general task-accuracy gains.

</details>


### [271] [Feature Reconfiguration With Visual Prior for Medical Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03535)
*Yinan Liu,Jiankang Hong,Zhen Gao,Ye Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FreNet框架，通过视觉先验和特征重构解决医学图像病变分割中的背景干扰和形态多样性问题，在9个基准上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于编码器-解码器的方法缺乏编码阶段的早期先验指导和特征重构，难以应对复杂背景干扰和多样病变形态的挑战。

Method: 提出FreNet框架，包含隐式先验神经网络（IPNN）在编码前进行像素级重构，以及双域特征重构（DFR）模块在编码中进行特征级重构；DFR内含频域解耦模块（FDM）和空间定位模块（SLM）。

Result: 在3种成像模态的9个医学图像分割基准上实验，FreNet显著优于SOTA方法；在ETIS数据集上Dice分数比SOTA提升5.0%，比SAM提升7.2%。

Conclusion: FreNet通过编码前的视觉先验引导和编码中的双域特征重构，有效提升了医学病变分割的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Lesion segmentation in medical images plays a critical role in clinical diagnosis and treatment planning. Despite significant advances, lesion segmentation remains challenging due to two major factors: (1) complex background interference; (2) diverse lesion morphology. Existing encoder-decoder based methods mainly focus on enhancing feature extraction or redesigning decoding strategies. However, they lack early prior guidance and feature reconfiguration during the encoding stage, limiting their effectiveness in handling these challenges. To address these limitations, we propose FreNet, a feature reconfiguration framework with visual priors, which performs pixel-level reconfiguration before encoding and feature-level reconfiguration during encoding for precise medical lesion segmentation. To suppress background responses, we propose an Implicit Prior Neural Network (IPNN), which models a continuous spatial field and leverages visual prior from SAM to reconfigure input image before encoding stage. To better handle diverse lesion morphology, we design a Dual-domain Feature Reconfiguration (DFR) module to progressively reconfigure backbone features during encoding stage. Within DFR, the Frequency Decoupling Module (FDM) decouples backbone features in frequency domain to enhance foreground-background discriminability, while the Spatial Localization Module (SLM) spatially relocates and improving spatial stability after frequency decoupling. Extensive experiments on 9 medical image segmentation benchmarks across three imaging modalities demonstrate that FreNet significantly outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. On the challenging ETIS dataset, our method achieves Dice improvements of 5.0% over SOTA method and 7.2% over SAM.

</details>


### [272] [A Prompt-Engineering Approach to Develop Scalable, Flexible, and Real-Time Hybrid Micro-Level Personalization in a General Purpose AI Teaching Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03402)
*Saptarshi Basu,Sandeep Kakar,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于提示工程的框架，用于个性化大型语言模型（LLM）驱动的教学助手，通过分析六个学习者维度和布鲁姆分类法来调整响应，无需重新训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI教学助手具有可扩展性，但往往缺乏个性化支持，需要一种无需重新训练模型即可实现跨学科和课程个性化的方法。

Method: 采用基于提示工程的框架，利用六个学习者特定维度（自我评估、抽象偏好、冗长偏好、感知导向、信息处理风格、理解水平）生成96种学习者档案，并结合布鲁姆分类法分析查询的认知复杂度，将属性编码到结构化提示中。

Result: 实验和五人人类研究显示，不同个性化条件下响应风格和结构存在感知差异，统计分析识别出与可测量响应变化相关的学习者属性。

Conclusion: 基于提示的个性化能够为LLM驱动的教育代理提供初步证据支持自适应行为，无需模型重新训练即可实现个性化教学支持。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) teaching assistants powered by large language models (LLMs) offer scalable educational support but often provide limited personalization. This study presents a prompt-engineering-based framework for personalizing general-purpose LLM/RAG-based AI teaching assistants such as Jill Watson across academic disciplines and courses. The framework adapts responses using six learner-specific dimensions: self-assessment, abstraction preference, verbosity preference, perceptual orientation, information processing style, and level of understanding, yielding 96 distinct learner profiles. Student queries are additionally analyzed using Bloom's Taxonomy to estimate cognitive complexity at the interaction level. Learner attributes and cognitive assessments are encoded in structured prompts that condition the LLM without requiring model retraining. The framework is evaluated through experiments using NLP metrics and a human study with five participants. Results show perceived differences in response style and structure across personalization conditions, with statistical analyses identifying learner attributes associated with measurable response changes. These findings provide preliminary evidence that prompt-based personalization can support adaptive behavior in LLM-powered educational agents.

</details>


### [273] [Caught in the Story: Narrative Captivity in Multi-turn LLMs Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03407)
*Yuhe Wu,Guangyu Wang,Yujie Chen,Jiatong Zhang,Yuran Chen,Yutong Zhang,Xiyin Cheng,Wenpeng Cao,Zhuang Liu,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了大型语言模型（LLM）在多轮道德咨询中存在的“叙事俘获”（narrative captivity）问题，即模型在缺乏对立观点的情况下，倾向于完全接受单方叙述并调整其判断。研究通过构建包含5,078个场景的基准测试发现，17个LLM平均判断偏移达25个百分点，偏好优化是主要成因，现有推理策略仅能部分缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多基于单轮判断或压力性反驳来评估LLM的道德建议能力，这与现实世界中人们寻求道德指导的方式不符。现实中道德冲突对话常涉及一方自我辩护的多轮叙述，导致信息不对称，但尚不清楚仅凭叙述（无明确对立立场）是否会在多轮咨询中改变模型判断。

Method: 提出“叙事俘获”概念，构建涵盖六个道德维度、包含5,078个人际冲突场景的基准测试。在17个LLM上进行多轮叙述实验，对比单轮基线，分析判断偏移情况，并进行阶段级分析以识别成因及测试推理时缓解策略。

Result: 在17个LLM中普遍存在叙事俘获现象：多轮叙述下的最终判断相比匹配的单轮基线平均偏移25个百分点。阶段级分析表明偏好优化是主要贡献因素，四种推理时策略仅提供部分缓解。

Conclusion: 叙事俘获是LLM在现实道德咨询中的重要失效模式，现有方法不足以完全解决该问题。研究旨在促进开发能在真实咨询中保持独立判断的LLM顾问系统。

Abstract: People increasingly turn to large language models (LLMs) for everyday advice, making ethically charged interpersonal problems a practical moral-advisory context. Most prior work has studied this context through single-turn judgments or pressure-laden rebuttals, assumptions that poorly match how guidance is sought in real-world contexts. These assumptions leave unclear whether narration alone, without an explicit opposing position, can shift model judgments during multi-turn moral consultation. Yet real-world moral-conflict conversation often elicits one party's self-justifying account, which can unfold over multiple turns and create information asymmetry. We introduce \textbf{narrative captivity}, a failure mode in which a model treats an unopposed one-sided account as complete and aligns with the narrator's interpretation without seeking missing perspectives. To measure this phenomenon, we build a benchmark of $5{,}078$ interpersonal-conflict scenarios spanning six moral dimensions. Across 17 LLMs, narrative captivity is widespread: end-state judgments under multi-turn narration shift by 25 percentage points on average beyond the matched single-turn baseline. Stage-level analysis identifies preference optimization as a major contributor, while four inference-time strategies provide only partial mitigation. We hope our project fosters LLM advisors that preserve independent judgment in real-world consultation.

</details>


### [274] [Dude: A Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for Paper-Code Discrepancy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03416)
*Weijie Liu,Running Zhao,Wenhao Yuan,Jinfeng Xu,Zhanfeng Xu,Xiaoxi Zhang,Edith Cheuk-Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dude，首个用于论文-代码差异检测的双检测多智能体系统，通过粒度对齐协商和两阶段显著性过滤机制，显著提升检测的召回率和精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着研究投稿量超过人工审核能力，LLM赋能的论文-代码差异检测受到关注，但现有单智能体LLM范式存在上下文容量有限和检测片面性问题，导致召回率表现不佳。

Method: 提出Dude双检测多智能体系统，设计粒度对齐协商机制和两阶段显著性过滤机制，解决论文语言与代码语言粒度不对称导致的过度解读和过度报告问题，减少假阳性。

Result: 在真实世界论文-代码差异数据集上的实验表明，Dude相比基线方法召回率和精确率最高提升22.8%，F1分数最高提升18.7%。

Conclusion: Dude通过创新的多智能体架构和机制设计，有效解决了现有方法的局限性，在论文-代码差异检测任务中实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: LLM-empowered paper-code discrepancy detection has received growing concern since the scaling of research submissions exceeds the manual review capability. However, the limited context capacity and one-sided discrepancy detection of existing single-agent LLM paradigms lead to an inferior recall performance in detecting discrepancies. In this paper, we propose Dude, the first Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for paper-code discrepancy detection. We discover that the granularity asymmetry of the paper-language and code-language introduces over-interpretation and over-reporting challenges in a multi-agent system design for discrepancy detection, resulting in increasing false positives. To address this, we propose a granularity-aligned negotiation and a two-stage salience-filtering mechanism in Dude, which effectively prevents agents from falsely reporting discrepancies. Experimental results in real-world paper-code discrepancy datasets showcase Dude's significant recall and precision improvement by up to 22.8%, increasing F1 score by up to 18.7% compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [275] [DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval: Evaluating Implicit Instruction Following in Full-Duplex Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03423)
*Puneet Mathur,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval) 基准，用于评估全双工语音代理在实时口语交互中的隐式指令遵循能力，发现不同架构的模型在遵循显式指令与角色暗示行为之间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全双工语音代理基准测试大多通过显式的轮转管理指令来测试行为，但实际部署中代理通常通过角色或人设配置，需要从中推断适当的对话行为，缺乏针对隐式指令遵循的评估基准。

Method: 构建包含 1,038 个测试用例的 DSB-IFEval 基准，涵盖 8 种不同的助手角色，评估 5 种指令遵循条件协议（默认行为、显式行为指令、人设暗示行为、人设 - 规则组合、指令冲突）。使用确定性指令遵循分数 (IAS) 测量实时轮转管理，使用 LLM 评判的人设遵循分数 (PAS) 评估内容一致性。

Result: 在 6 个实时语音系统中发现架构依赖的权衡：全双工模型 (F-Actor, PersonaPlex) 对人设仅条件更敏感，遵循度分别下降 9.7% 和 4.5%；GPT-Realtime 等模型在人设内容上遵循良好，但轮转行为无法适应不同指令类型。即使系统能遵循与人设冲突的指令，在安全冲突下仍难以覆盖人设。

Conclusion: 推断角色暗示的行为、在适当的对话时刻执行该行为、以及解决竞争指令，对于全双工语音代理来说仍然是三个不同的挑战。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents must continuously decide when to listen, backchannel, interrupt, handle speech overlaps, take the floor, and yield. Existing benchmarks largely test these behaviors through explicit turn-management instructions, while deployed agents are often configured through roles or personas from which the appropriate conversational behavior must be inferred. We introduce DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval) for evaluating implicit instruction-following in real-time spoken interaction. (DSB-IFEval) comprises 1,038 test cases spanning eight diverse assistant roles and evaluates five conditioning protocols for instruction-following: default behavior, explicit behavioral instructions, persona-implied behavior, combined persona--rule conditioning, and instruction conflict. We measure real-time floor management using a deterministic Instruction Adherence Score (IAS) and persona-consistent content using LLM-judged Persona Adherence Score (PAS). Across six real-time speech systems, we find architecture-dependent trade-offs. Full duplex models like F-Actor and PersonaPlex are more sensitive to whether conversational behavior is stated explicitly or must be inferred from a persona, with adherence dropping by 9.7% and 4.5%, respectively, under persona-only conditioning. In contrast, GPT-Realtime, MiniCPM-o, and Fun-Audio-Chat strongly adhere to persona-consistent content, but their floor behavior does not adapt across explicit and persona-only instructions and remains constrained on several proactive actions. We further find that even if systems reliably follow conflicting directives to their prescribed persona, they still struggle to override them under safety conflict. These results show that inferring the behavior implied by a role, executing it at the appropriate conversational moment, and resolving competing instructions remain distinct challenges for full-duplex voice agents.

</details>


### [276] [Counterfactual Routing Using Integer Programming with Constraint Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03707)
*Daniël Vos,Sterre Lutz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提交至 IJCAI 2025 反事实路由竞赛，通过整数规划模型求解最短路径问题的反事实解释，在保持第四名解质量的同时，以平均 9.0 秒的运行速度远超其他参赛方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决最短路径问题中的反事实解释需求，即确定对路网进行何种最小改动可使用户选择的路线成为最优路线，从而提供如“若 X 道路非自行车道，则建议路线确为最优”的可解释性说明。

Method: 将问题建模为整数规划（Integer Program），采用迭代方式逐步加入约束条件，直至找到精确解。

Result: 在预留测试实例的最终评估中，该方法解质量排名第四，但在所有实例上求解速度最快，平均运行时间为 9.0 秒，而次快方案平均耗时 118.8 秒。

Conclusion: 所提出的整数规划迭代约束方法在保证较高解质量的前提下，显著提升了反事实路由问题的求解效率，具有优异的实时性能表现。

Abstract: We present our submission to the IJCAI 2025 'Counterfactual Routing Competition' (CRC 25). The goal of the competition is to find counterfactual explanations for the shortest path problem. This requires deciding what the minimal changes to a road network would make a route chosen by the user the optimal route. This enables explanations such as "Your suggested route would indeed have been optimal, if road X were not a bicycle path." Our solution models the problem as an integer program, iteratively incorporating constraints until an exact solution is found. In the final evaluation on held-out test instances, our method ranked fourth in solution quality and obtained its solution fastest on every instance, with an average runtime of 9.0 seconds compared to 118.8 seconds for the next-fastest submission.

</details>


### [277] [Do GUI Agents Know When Not to Act? Enabling Conflict-Aware Termination for Multimodal GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03438)
*Zhaoyuan Huang,Tianjie Ju,Pengzhou Cheng,Zheng Wu,Yansi Li,Chuanbiao Song,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对GUI代理在面对用户不可行指令时盲目执行的问题，提出了CONFLICTGUI基准测试和CONFLICTGUARD推理时框架，显著提升了代理识别冲突并适时终止任务的能力，同时保持了正常任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GUI代理在执行自然语言指令时，往往表现出“执行偏向的过度顺从”（execution-biased overcompliance），即面对包含内部逻辑冲突或与GUI上下文冲突的不可行指令时，代理仍会盲目执行而非停止，缺乏对“何时不行动”的判断能力。

Method: 1. 构建CONFLICTGUI基准：涵盖指令内部冲突和指令-GUI上下文冲突，用于评估冲突感知终止能力。2. 提出CONFLICTGUARD框架：包含两个耦合组件，一是可行性验证协议（指导代理在行动前评估指令逻辑和GUI证据），二是条件动作调制机制（引导代理从过度执行转向终止导向行为）。

Result: 在五个广泛使用的GUI代理上的实验表明，CONFLICTGUARD显著提高了冲突任务的成功率，同时保留了正常GUI任务的性能，证明了轻量级推理时干预的有效性。

Conclusion: 轻量级的推理时干预能大幅提升GUI代理识别不适宜执行场景的能力，使其在面临冲突指令时能够 refrain from unnecessary actions（避免不必要的操作），从而实现更可靠的人机交互。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents are increasingly used to execute natural-language instructions on user interfaces, yet real users may issue infeasible instructions due to benign mistakes. A reliable agent should not only know how to act, but also when not to act. In this work, we introduce CONFLICTGUI, a benchmark covering instruction-internal conflicts and instruction-GUI context conflicts to study conflict-aware termination. Our evaluation reveals severe execution-biased overcompliance: agents that perform well on feasible tasks often continue to execute blindly under conflicting instructions. To mitigate this behavior, we propose CONFLICTGUARD, an inference-time framework that aligns an agent's feasibility awareness with its action generation. CONFLICTGUARD contains two coupled components: a feasibility verification protocol that guides the agent to assess instruction logic and GUI-side evidence before acting, and a conditional action modulation mechanism that steers agents from over-compliant execution into termination-oriented behavior. Experiments across five widely-used agents demonstrate that CONFLICTGUARD improves average conflict task success rate significantly, while preserving normal GUI-task performance. These results validate that a lightweight inference-time intervention can substantially boost GUI Agent's competence to identify inappropriate execution scenarios and refrain from unnecessary actions.

</details>


### [278] [Beyond "Made with AI": Visualizing Provenance Density to Mitigate the Transparency Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03460)
*Qing Zhang,Yifei Huang,Juyoung Lee,Thad Starner,Jun Rekimoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对生成式AI导致的“流畅性陷阱”，本文提出“溯源密度”（Provenance Density）证据可视化界面，取代传统的“AI生成”标签。研究显示，该界面能显著提升用户辨别真伪的能力，且技术审计发现“一致性否决”（Consistency Veto）在动态查询中比单纯的检索密度更具判别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使得流畅的文本生产变得廉价，导致用户陷入“流畅性陷阱”：既信任流畅的幻觉，又在得知内容为AI生成后低估准确信息。传统的二元“AI生成”标签仅披露作者身份，无法展示支撑主张的证据，因此需要更有效的透明度机制。

Method: 1. 提出“溯源密度”概念，设计一种展示文本中已验证主张密度的证据可视化界面。
2. 进行包含81名参与者的用户研究，对比理想化界面与无信号条件下的用户辨别能力。
3. 对200个样本进行技术审计，评估检索密度与一致性否决（Consistency Veto）在动态查询中的判别信号强度。

Result: 1. 用户研究显示，使用理想化溯源密度界面后，用户对真相与伪造的辨别差距显著扩大（+4.15分，效应量d=1.82），而无信号组未表现出辨别能力。
2. 技术审计表明，仅靠检索密度不足以保证效果；意外发现“一致性否决”机制在动态查询中承载了主要的判别信号。

Conclusion: 随着AI生成内容与人类写作日益难以区分，有效的透明度机制必须从单纯的作者身份披露转向证据可视化，以帮助用户识别内容真伪。

Abstract: As generative AI makes polished prose cheap to produce, users can no longer rely on fluency as a proxy for truth. We call this failure mode the Fluency Trap: users trust fluent hallucinations while also discounting accurate content once it is disclosed as AI-generated. Binary ``Made with AI'' labels respond with authorship disclosure, but they do not show what supports a claim. We propose Provenance Density, an evidence-visualization interface that shows the density of verified claims in a text. In a user study with 81 participants, an idealized Provenance Density interface produced a large discernment gap between truth and fabrication ($+4.15$ points, $d=1.82$), whereas participants given no signal showed no detectable discrimination. A technical audit with 200 samples shows that retrieval density alone is insufficient; unexpectedly, the Consistency Veto carries most of the discriminative signal on dynamic queries. As AI-generated content becomes indistinguishable from human writing, effective transparency must move from authorship disclosure toward evidence visualization.

</details>


### [279] [AutoGraphForge: Towards Automated Graph Theory Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03478)
*Ján Pastorek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为AutoGraphForge的自动化图论猜想生成、反驳、形式化与证明系统，通过多轮迭代生成6522个非平凡猜想，并结合神经证明器在Lean 4中完成形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一个端到端的自动化系统，解决图论中猜想生成效率低、验证困难及形式化证明复杂的问题，提升数学发现的自动化水平。

Method: 采用反例引导的猜想生成机制，结合Graffiti3生成器、新颖性过滤器（基于559个经典关系）、大规模图数据集测试（约34.8万图）及反例搜索算法；形式化阶段使用Lean 4将猜想翻译为形式语句，并集成DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B和OProver-32B两个神经证明器进行内核验证。

Result: 系统运行多轮后生成6522个通过所有过滤和测试的猜想，其中包括二分图和正则图中消解数与边覆盖数之间的非平凡关系，并已完成部分手动证明及Lean 4形式化验证。

Conclusion: AutoGraphForge展示了自动化图论猜想发现与证明的可行性，为数学研究的自动化提供了有效框架，目前系统已在高性能计算集群上全面运行。

Abstract: We report on our ongoing project to develop a computational pipeline, AutoGraphForge, for an automated graph-theoretic conjecturing-refuting-formalizing-proving system. Conjecture generation is counterexample-guided and runs in rounds: a Graffiti3 generator proposes conjectures over a small, evolving snapshot table $T$ (initially a few hundred graphs with their computed invariants) that grows only by counterexamples to its own conjectures. A novelty filter of $559$ classical and folklore relations, closed under transitive composition and linear identity substitution, decides via a linear program whether a candidate is already implied by known results. Surviving candidates are tested against a dataset of about $348,000$ graphs, unioning the complete House of Graphs invariant export, the exhaustive census of all connected graphs on at most nine vertices, several extremal families (strongly regular, minimal Ramsey, Cayley, cages, barbells, lollipops, spiders), and random models. Counterexample-search algorithms then attack the remainder. Run for several rounds on an HPC cluster, the loop yields $6,522$ conjectures that survived the refutation dataset, the novelty filter and every active-search run -- among them nontrivial relations between the annihilation number and the edge-cover number for bipartite and regular graphs, which we prove by hand. A subsequent formalization and proving stage deterministically translates each surviving conjecture into a Lean 4 statement skeleton; every candidate proof is kernel-verified against a pinned mathlib4 and our custom invariant preamble. This stage integrates two neural provers -- DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B (served with vLLM) and the Lean-specialised OProver-32B -- behind the independent kernel check. It is implemented end-to-end and passes initial sanity checks, with the full pipeline currently running on the cluster.

</details>


### [280] [Making Every Tool Call Count: Necessary Tool-Evidence Path Rewards for Agentic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03493)
*Xingming Long,Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Hanqi Feng,Shaojie Zhang,Barnabas Poczos,Chao Jiang,Zhenbo Luo,Lei Jiang,Pei Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NTEP（必要工具 - 证据路径）标注方案及NTEP-R奖励机制，通过细粒度监督工具调用与证据获取过程，解决了现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在复杂查询中工具调用冗余及信息提取失败的问题，显著提升了搜索准确率与工具使用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代理式VLM训练范式仅基于最终答案的正确性评估工具使用，缺乏对证据获取和利用过程的充分监督，导致模型常发出冗余或偏离目标的工具调用，且无法从观测结果中提取必要信息。

Method: 提出NTEP标注方案，明确指定每个查询所需的外部证据及对应工具调用；设计NTEP-R监督机制，奖励代理将调用前意图与必要证据寻求目标对齐，并确保调用后总结的信息与必要证据一致；引入非重复目标正则化器，惩罚重复已满足目标的冗余调用。

Result: 在七个图像 grounding 基准测试上的评估显示，8B参数的NTEP-8B模型在统一的三工具框架下，显著提高了面向搜索的准确性和工具使用效率。

Conclusion: 细粒度的工具 - 证据路径监督对于训练鲁棒的代理式视觉语言模型具有关键价值。

Abstract: Modern vision-language models (VLMs) can directly answer many image-grounded questions, yet they often struggle with complex queries requiring fine-grained visual details or external knowledge. To acquire this missing evidence, agentic VLMs invoke tools such as image cropping, image search, and text search. However, existing training paradigms primarily evaluate tool-use based on final answer correctness, leaving evidence acquisition and utilization insufficiently supervised. This leads to two critical shortcomings: (i) models frequently issue redundant or off-target tool calls that fail to gather necessary evidence, and (ii) even when appropriate tools are called, models often fail to extract the necessary information from the resulting observations. To address these limitations, we introduce the NTEP (Necessary Tool-Evidence Path), a novel annotation scheme that explicitly specifies the essential external evidence and corresponding tool calls for each query. Building upon this, we propose NTEP-R (NTEP Reward), a supervision mechanism ensuring that each tool invocation strictly advances the reasoning process toward the final solution. Specifically, our approach rewards the agent for aligning its pre-call intent with a necessary evidence-seeking goal, and for ensuring the information summarized from the post-call observation aligns with the necessary evidence. Furthermore, we introduce a non-repeated-goal regularizer to penalize redundant calls that revisit satisfied NTEP goals. Extensive evaluations on seven image-grounded benchmarks demonstrate that our 8B-parameter instantiation, NTEP-8B, significantly improves both search-oriented accuracy and tool-use efficiency within a unified three-tool framework. These results highlight the critical value of fine-grained tool-evidence path supervision for training robust agentic VLMs.

</details>


### [281] [GrowPage: On-Demand KV Budgeting for Efficient LLM Reasoning Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03494)
*Qiankun Ma,Yanjiang Zhou,Zinan Xiong,Haofei Wang,Zhen Song,Yang Xiang,Ziyao Zhang,Hairong Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对长文本推理中KV缓存的内存瓶颈，提出了GrowPage框架。该方法摒弃了固定的每请求预算，通过双时间尺度查询摘要动态评估注意力需求，按需分配或压缩物理页面，从而在保持连续批处理和前缀缓存的同时，实现了优于现有方法的性能与吞吐量平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV压缩方法通常依赖预定义的每请求预算，且在整个解码过程中保持总容量固定。然而，推理工作负载的需求波动巨大：不同请求所需的KV容量不同，且单个请求的注意力需求在生成过程中会动态演变，固定容量策略无法有效应对这种变化。

Method: 提出GrowPage，一种按需KV预算框架，将KV容量视为运行时资源。该方法维护轻量级的双时间尺度查询摘要（捕捉近期和长期注意力行为），利用相对注意力工作集估计需求演变。在容量边界处，根据需求选择在当前分配内压缩KV状态或获取额外的物理页面。该框架集成于PagedAttention的页面级内存抽象中，以保留连续批处理和前缀缓存功能。

Result: 在多个模型的推理基准测试中，GrowPage相比现有方法实现了更优的性能与吞吐量权衡（performance-throughput trade-off）。

Conclusion: 通过将KV容量动态化管理并集成到页面级内存抽象中，GrowPage有效解决了长输出推理中的内存瓶颈问题，显著提升了LLM服务效率。

Abstract: Long-output reasoning has made the key--value (KV) cache a critical memory bottleneck for efficient LLM serving. Existing KV compression methods usually rely on a predefined per-request budget and adjust only which KV states are retained, leaving the total capacity fixed throughout decoding. However, reasoning workloads exhibit substantial demand variation: different requests require different KV capacities, and the attention demand of an individual request evolves during generation. We introduce \textbf{GrowPage}, an on-demand KV budgeting framework that treats KV capacity as a runtime resource. GrowPage maintains lightweight dual-timescale query summaries to capture recent and long-term attention behaviors, and uses their relative attention working sets to estimate demand evolution. At each capacity boundary, GrowPage either compresses KV states within the current allocation or acquires an additional physical page when broader demand emerges. By integrating with PagedAttention's page-level memory abstraction, GrowPage preserves continuous batching and prefix caching. Experiments on reasoning benchmarks across multiple models show that GrowPage achieves a superior performance--throughput trade-off over existing approaches.

</details>


### [282] [PPO-STGNN: A Proximal Policy Optimization Approach with Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks for DAG Task Scheduling in Cloud-Edge-End Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03503)
*Yangshuo Qi,Chenwei Wang,Zihan Shen,Songlin Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PPO-STGNN的DAG任务调度算法，通过结合近端策略优化（PPO）和时空图神经网络（STGNN），解决了云 - 边 - 端协同环境中异构资源下计算密集型DAG任务的高效调度问题，实验表明该算法在保持低完成时间的同时显著改善了负载均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物联网的发展，云 - 边 - 端协同环境中计算密集型DAG任务日益普遍，但云、边、端节点在计算能力、网络带宽和能耗方面高度异构，使得具有复杂依赖关系的任务高效调度成为NP难问题。传统启发式算法和常规强化学习方法往往无法捕捉系统资源的时空动态特性。

Method: 提出PPO-STGNN算法，利用STGNN从DAG任务拓扑和物理云 - 边 - 端资源图中提取特征，然后通过PPO优化调度策略以最小化完工时间和调度长度比（SLR），同时改善CPU和内存负载均衡；引入多教师行为克隆机制进行预训练以加速收敛。

Result: 实验结果表明，PPO-STGNN在保持较低完成时间的同时，显著改善了负载均衡性能。

Conclusion: PPO-STGNN算法适用于动态和异构的云 - 边 - 端DAG调度场景，能有效解决复杂依赖任务的高效调度问题。

Abstract: With the rapid development of the Internet of Things, computation intensive directed acyclic graph (DAG) tasks have become increasingly common in cloud-edge-end collaborative environments. However, cloud, edge, and end nodes are highly heterogeneous in computing capacity, network bandwidth, and energy consumption, which makes the efficient scheduling of tasks with complex dependencies an NP-hard problem. Traditional heuristic algorithms and conventional reinforcement-learning methods often fail to capture the spatio-temporal dynamics of system resources. This paper proposes PPO-STGNN, a DAG task-scheduling algorithm that integrates proximal policy optimization (PPO) with spatio-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs). The method uses an STGNN to extract features from both the DAG task topology and the physical cloud-edge-end resource graph, and then optimizes the scheduling policy through PPO to minimize makespan and schedule length ratio (SLR) while improving CPU and memory load balancing. To accelerate convergence, a multi-teacher behavior-cloning mechanism is introduced for pretraining. Experimental results show that PPO-STGNN significantly improves load balancing while maintaining a low completion time, making it suitable for dynamic and heterogeneous cloud-edge- end DAG scheduling scenarios.

</details>


### [283] [What Matters for Aggressive Decoding-Time KV Eviction? Temporal Aggregation and Ranking Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03515)
*Bo Zeng,Yu Zhao,Yefeng Liu,Zhihong Lu,Xuanfan Ni,Xintong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 解码时KV缓存压缩研究过度关注评分函数设计，而忽略了时间聚合规则。本文发现EMA聚合使评分修改在驱逐集层面几乎不可区分，并提出InertiaKV方法，显著提升解码吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解码时KV缓存压缩研究过于侧重设计更好的token评分函数，而将跨解码步骤聚合分数的时间规则视为实现细节，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 分析不同评分函数在EMA聚合下的表现，提出InertiaKV（基于EMA的解码时驱逐方法）及其变体InertiaKV-Lazy（定期刷新版本），并研究Score-Free解码作为独立操作点。

Result: EMA聚合下，Value-norm和熵变体保持高度相关，而KeyDiff等评分函数排名改变且性能下降显著。InertiaKV-Lazy相比完整刷新版本获得1.34-1.46倍解码吞吐量提升，Score-Free解码平均质量变化+0.03。

Conclusion: 时间聚合和排名保留是独立且关键的设计因素，评分质量并非在所有情况下都至关重要，为KV缓存压缩提供了新的设计视角。

Abstract: Decoding-time KV cache compression research focuses heavily on designing better token scoring functions, while the temporal rule that aggregates scores across decode steps is often treated as an implementation detail. Under aggressive KV compression, we find that exponential-moving-average (EMA) aggregation makes approximately order-preserving scorer modifications largely indistinguishable at the eviction-set level. Value-norm and entropy variants remain highly correlated with attention and produce nearly unchanged retention sets, whereas KeyDiff, key norm, recency, and a learned scorer alter the ranking and degrade substantially. We associate this stability with the evaluated aggregation, which couples layer weighting and temporal retention. Building on this observation, we introduce InertiaKV, an EMA-based decoding-time eviction method, and InertiaKV-Lazy, its periodic-refresh variant, which yields 1.34-1.46x decode throughput relative to full refresh InertiaKV. We also study Score-Free decoding as a separate empirical operating point: it scores the full context once at the first decode step, freezes that ranking, and incurs an average quality change of +0.03 while removing all subsequent scoring. Across six open-weight backbones and the LongBench, LongBench-v2, and RULER benchmarks, the results identify temporal aggregation and ranking preservation as distinct, consequential design factors; they do not imply that scoring quality is irrelevant in general.

</details>


### [284] [CulturalMenuBench: Probing the Knowledge-Application Gap in Multimodal Culinary Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03526)
*Bo Zeng,Linfeng Gao,Peiqin Lin,Yu Zhao,Mingyan Zeng,Yu Tong,Xintong Wang,Linlong Xu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo,Qinggang Zhang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CulturalMenuBench基准，揭示多模态语言模型在食物识别上虽得分高，但缺乏真正的文化理解，无法将视觉输入与文化知识有效关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在食物识别基准上表现优异，但尚不清楚这是源于真正的文化理解还是单纯的视觉匹配，需探究模型是否具备将视觉感知与文化背景知识相结合的能力。

Method: 构建包含10种语言、18个地区共4,870个条目的CulturalMenuBench基准，设计10项任务，结合成品菜与步骤图、食材、流程文本及地区标签，评估12个模型在从基础识别到过程 grounded 文化归因上的表现，并通过诊断分析与消融实验验证任务有效性。

Result: 模型在标准多选题中准确率超94%，但在中餐地区归因任务中降至最高56%；错误模式接近随机猜测，准确率依赖视觉显著性而非文化结构；仅凭菜名分类比结合图像高7-18分；消融实验证实移除步骤图会显著影响过程 grounded 任务。

Conclusion: 高识别率掩盖了模型应用文化知识的缺陷，视觉输入无法激活已有知识，需通过显式连接感知、流程与文化上下文的训练策略加以改进。

Abstract: Multimodal language models achieve near-ceiling scores on food recognition benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether this success reflects genuine cultural understanding or mere visual matching. To probe this distinction, we introduce CulturalMenuBench, a benchmark of 4,870 items in 10 languages across 18 regions; its 10 tasks pair final-dish and step-by-step cooking images with ingredients, procedural text, and regional labels, spanning basic recognition to process-grounded cultural attribution. Evaluating 12 models exposes a substantial knowledge-application gap: models exceeding 94% on standard multiple-choice tasks drop to at most 56% when attributing dishes to Chinese regional cuisines, despite an identical four-way format. Diagnostic analyses explain why: error patterns are consistent with random guessing, accuracy tracks visual distinctiveness rather than cultural structure, and models classify cuisines more accurately from dish names alone than from images (+7-18 points). The knowledge is thus present but cannot be activated through visual input. An ablation confirms these tasks genuinely require procedural evidence: removing sequential cooking images selectively degrades process-grounded tasks while others remain stable. Overall, CulturalMenuBench shows that near-perfect recognition can conceal an inability to apply cultural knowledge, motivating training that explicitly connects perception, procedure, and cultural context. Code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [285] [NeoRed: A Knowledge-Logic-Alignment Multimodal Large Language Model for Neonatal Respiratory Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03527)
*Yinan Liu,Hongtai Xia,Haoran Xu,Jiankang Hong,Jingkuan Song,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对新生儿呼吸疾病诊断中多模态大语言模型（MLLM）存在的领域差距和临床上下文整合不足问题，提出了首个专为新生儿设计的MLLM——NeoRed。通过构建NeoCXR和NeoCXR-EV两个真实临床数据集，并设计知识 - 逻辑 - 对齐（KLA）框架（包含知识先验注入、诊断逻辑约束和视觉语义对齐），实现了准确的新生儿诊断报告生成，在NeoCXR数据集上ROUGE-L达53.29%，临床效能F1分数达65.19%，优于现有MLLM，同时在成人基准测试中保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿呼吸疾病是新生儿发病和死亡的主要原因，临床诊断挑战巨大。现有MLLM在新生儿诊断中存在两大局限：一是训练数据以成人为主导致的领域差距；二是缺乏对多维临床上下文的有效整合以支持准确诊断。

Method: 1. 收集两个真实世界临床数据集NeoCXR和NeoCXR-EV；2. 提出NeoRed模型，首个专为新生儿呼吸疾病设计的MLLM；3. 设计知识 - 逻辑 - 对齐（KLA）框架，包含三个核心组件：知识先验注入（KPI）将新生儿专家诊断先验融入多模态表示，引导跨模态疾病特异性注意力；诊断逻辑约束（DLC）使生成报告语义与多模态诊断逻辑对齐；视觉语义对齐（VSA）建立视觉特征与影像结论间的语义对应关系。

Result: 在NeoCXR数据集上，NeoRed实现ROUGE-L 53.29%和临床效能F1分数65.19%，性能优于现有MLLM；同时在成人基准数据集（MIMIC-CXR和IU-Xray）上保持具有竞争力的报告生成性能。

Conclusion: NeoRed成功解决了新生儿诊断中MLLM的领域差距和临床上下文整合问题，实现了准确的新生儿诊断报告生成，填补了该领域空白，且具备跨年龄段泛化能力，相关数据集可申请获取。

Abstract: Neonatal respiratory diseases are a major cause of neonatal morbidity and mortality, posing substantial challenges in clinical practice. Despite recent advances, existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face two key limitations in neonatal diagnosis: (1) domain gap arising from predominantly adult training data; (2) insufficient integration of multidimensional clinical context for accurate diagnosis. To address these challenges, we collect two real-world clinical datasets (NeoCXR and NeoCXR-EV) and propose NeoRed, to the best of our knowledge, the first MLLM tailored for neonatal respiratory disease, filling the gap in neonatal diagnostic reports generation. To enhance joint diagnosis from heterogeneous clinical context and chest X-rays, we design a novel Knowledge-Logic-Alignment (KLA) framework which constrains model behavior from three perspectives: 1) Knowledge Prior Injection (KPI) incorporates neonatologist-inspired diagnostic priors into multimodal representations, guiding disease-specific attention across modalities; 2) Diagnostic Logic Constraint (DLC) aligns the semantics of generated reports with multimodal diagnostic logic; and 3) Visual Semantic Alignment (VSA) establishes semantic correspondence between visual features and imaging conclusions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NeoRed enables accurate neonatal diagnostic reports generation, achieving ROUGE-L of 53.29% and Clinical Efficacy F1 score of 65.19% on NeoCXR, outperforming existing MLLMs. NeoRed also preserves competitive report generation performance on adult benchmarks (MIMIC-CXR and IU-Xray). Datasets will be available upon application.

</details>


### [286] [Dalek: A Constructive Agent Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03546)
*Wanpeng Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Dalek是一个基于冯·诺依曼自复制自动机设计的封闭机器，通过三个原语（参与者、消息、通道）和四个义务实现自维护、自进化、自复制和自组织，并将大语言模型与编译器作为有效载荷，实现能力的自主生成与继承。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一种能够在满足通用主机契约的任何基质上实现自我维持、自我进化、自我复制和自我组织的封闭机器系统，解决现有代理系统缺乏结构性自组织和持续进化能力的问题。

Method: 基于冯·诺依曼1948年自复制自动机的遗传构造核心（自描述、构造器、复制器、控制器），结合三个原语（参与者、消息、通道）和四个义务（主机边界、构造语言、允许转换、规则遗传），重构文本-消息代理基质，添加边界、身份、历史和生长的显式结构，将大语言模型和编译器作为有效载荷生成新能力。

Result: 成功设计出Dalek封闭机器架构，实现了能力的自主编写、编译、安装到描述中并由后代继承，机器能够生成自身器官甚至运行时环境，在机器内部完成了遗传和进化的闭环。

Conclusion: Dalek通过结合冯·诺依曼自复制核心与四个结构性义务，在通用主机契约下实现了代理系统的自组织、自进化和自复制，为构建具有持续进化能力的自主代理系统提供了理论基础和架构框架。

Abstract: We present Dalek, a closed machine designed for agents that realizes self-maintenance, self-evolution, self-reproduction, and self-organization on any substrate satisfying a general host contract. The machine is built from three primitives---actors, messages, and channels. Four obligations---a host boundary, a construction language, admissible transitions, and rule heredity---give its boundary, identity, and closure a structural basis.
  Von Neumann's 1948 self-reproducing automaton supplies a hereditary constructional core: a self-description together with a constructor, a copier, and a controller. Dalek combines this core with the four obligations and rederives its medium for a text-and-message agent substrate, adding explicit structures for boundary, identity, history, and growth. A large language model and a compiler occupy the payload position and form a general capability producer. New capabilities are authored, compiled, installed into the description, and inherited by descendants. The same path produces the machine's own organs and even its runtime, closing heredity and evolution within the machine.

</details>


### [287] [GPS-Bench: A Governance Policy Benchmark for Automating Policy Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03553)
*Linh Le,Melanie Bui,My Chiffon Nguyen,Zachary Schlosser,David Williams-King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GPS-Bench，一个基于证据的政策模拟基准，通过立法记录、游说披露等公共证据链接政策、参与者行为及下游影响，评估LLM在政策分析中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM政策模拟缺乏与真实结果的对比验证，难以评估其有效性；需要建立基于真实证据的基准来检验多智能体模拟在政策预测和解释中的价值。

Method: 构建GPS-Bench基准，从 dated record 重建参与者而非原型提示，使用人工标注数据作为Gold评估集，LLM标注数据作为Silver监督；对比联合推理、独立/通信智能体、图方法及权重微调等多种推断模式。

Result: 在真实记录上微调可获得最强的参与者级影响预测效果，分解方法虽未超越微调但提供了机制解释；多智能体可基于私有证据形成可验证的联盟承诺。

Conclusion: GPS-Bench为研究证据、参与者建模和多智能体交互如何提升政策结果预测与解释提供了统一的实证环境。

Abstract: Policy analysis requires more than predicting whether a proposal will pass: it requires identifying who will be affected, how those actors respond, and what follows. LLM-based policy simulations model these processes at scale, but their validity is hard to establish when plausible behaviour is never compared with observed outcomes. We introduce GPS-Bench, an evidence-grounded benchmark for governance policy simulation that links policies to relevant actors, actor actions and downstream impacts using legislative records, lobbying disclosures, regulatory documents, corporate filings, economic data and other public evidence. Actors are reconstructed from the dated record rather than prompted as archetypes, so a persona is an evidence object with provenance; a human-annotated pool forms the Gold evaluation set, while cases labelled by a separate LLM from retrieved evidence are treated as Silver supervision and never as test labels. Because every inference mode reads the same grounded state and emits the same schema, GPS-Bench turns "does multi-agent simulation help?" into a controlled comparison: we contrast joint reasoning, independent and communicating actor agents, graph-based methods and weight-level fine-tuning over one policy state. Fine-tuning on the grounded record gives the strongest actor-level impact prediction, and decomposition does not beat it; what decomposition adds is mechanism. Agents hold private, non-identical evidence, each seeing its own exposure clause, and address named partners with concrete joint proposals, what they offer, what they need in return, and why acting together beats acting alone, so the coalitions that form can be checked against the commitments the record holds. GPS-Bench therefore gives a common empirical setting for studying when evidence, actor modelling and multi-agent interaction improve the prediction and interpretation of policy outcomes.

</details>


### [288] [HalluPeer: A Taxonomy-driven Benchmark for Detecting Hallucinations in Scientific Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03580)
*Tzu-Ling Lin,Dong-Ting Yao,Teng-Fang Hsiao,Wei-Chih Chen,Hong-Han Shuai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HalluPeer，一个专为科学同行评审设计的幻觉检测基准，旨在解决现有基准无法在长篇技术论文中验证LLM生成评审主张的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着学术同行评审规模扩大，大语言模型（LLMs）被用作评审助手，但其生成的流畅却无依据的主张（幻觉）会削弱评审的可靠性。现有的幻觉基准未针对同行评审场景设计，缺乏在长篇技术论文中进行事实核查（grounding）的能力。

Method: 构建了包含论文内容、人工撰写评审及注入幻觉的评审的对齐三元组数据集。通过建立同行评审特有的幻觉分类法，识别评审上下文，并利用自动化过滤技术注入幻觉，从而创建用于检测、分类和定位的标注数据。

Result: 在12K篇论文和38K篇评审上的实验表明，现有检测器难以区分幻觉与合理的批判性意见；对真实评审的评估证实，HalluPeer定义的幻觉模式确实存在于实际同行评审中。

Conclusion: 现有检测工具在同行评审场景下表现不足，真实评审中存在显著的幻觉风险，因此亟需开发具备来源感知（source-aware）验证能力的检测机制。

Abstract: The growing scale of academic peer review has motivated the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) as review assistants, yet LLMs can generate fluent but unsupported claims that undermine review reliability. Existing hallucination benchmarks are not designed for peer review, where verification requires grounding claims in long, technical papers. We introduce HalluPeer, a benchmark for detecting hallucinations in scientific peer reviews, providing aligned triples of paper content, human-written reviews, and hallucination-injected reviews, annotated for detection, classification, and localization. Our pipeline induces a peer-review-specific hallucination taxonomy, identifies review contexts, and injects hallucinations with automated filtering. Experiments on 12K papers and 38K reviews show that existing detectors struggle to separate hallucinations from legitimate critique, while evaluation on authentic reviews demonstrates that HalluPeer-defined hallucination patterns occur in real peer reviews, highlighting the critical need for source-aware verification. Our project page can be found in https://github.com/Lin-TzuLing/HalluPeer.git

</details>


### [289] [The Attention Triangle in Audio-Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03586)
*Sagi Polaczek,Noa Kraicer,Gal Metzer,Zhuo Ning,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri,Daniel Cohen-Or,Raja Giryes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了音视频扩散模型中跨模态注意力机制导致的语义泄漏问题，发现音频 - 视频边存在双向路由且受模型偏差影响，提出了基于注意力信号的诊断工具及推理时干预方法，有效提升了语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频扩散模型依赖跨模态注意力协调文本、声音和视觉内容，但该机制可能引入微妙且系统的语义泄漏，当提示词与模型先验冲突时，会导致生成结果偏离预期，亟需深入分析其内部路由机制并加以干预。

Method: 通过探测和分析连接文本、音频、视频流的“注意力三角形”，提取注意力衍生信号作为诊断工具，分析语义在各模态间的分布与接地情况，并据此设计推理时干预策略以优化跨模态对齐。

Result: 实验表明，音频 - 视频边的双向路由是语义泄漏的主要来源，受模型参数偏差驱动；利用提取的注意力信号进行干预后，在保持生成质量的同时显著改善了语义接地效果。

Conclusion: 语义伪影不仅源于注意力扩散，更源于特定路径上结构化、偏差驱动的交互；通过注意力信号诊断并干预跨模态路由，可有效提升音视频生成模型的语义一致性与可控性。

Abstract: Audio-video diffusion models rely on cross-modal attention to coordinate text, sound, and visual content, yet this same mechanism can introduce subtle and systematic semantic leakage. We study these models by probing and analyzing the ``attention triangle,'' comprising the three cross-attention edges connecting the text, audio, and video streams, and examine how semantic information is routed across modalities during generation. Our analysis reveals that routing along the audio-video edge is bidirectional: audio can influence video generation, while video can influence audio generation.
